The pre-init actual-VFS-handle proofs bind SQLite's source/destination handles; during successful init-to-finish no destination-connection API is used for identity checking. Immediately after finish and before either close, `AI_BRAIN_FCNTL_FILE_IDENTITY_V1` is repeated for both handles and must still equal retained descriptors and broker-fixed-leaf identities. Before provisional and again during the final ready-release revalidation, the worker additionally requires checked `now + reservation_watchdog_takeover_headroom_ms + reservation_rename_headroom_ms < copy_finish_by`.

The producer's canonical opener locks and the source-registry postterminal freeze have deliberately different lifetimes. Both remain held through the installed provisional successor and the worker's final complete revalidation of that exact successor. Only then may the source-writer supervisor install and acknowledge the ready `freeze_release_v1` successor. The canonical opener locks remain held through the final copy-success transition, so no sanitizer or later raw-stage role can bind between provisional and committed state. After the ready-release acknowledgement, a registered source writer may begin and change later source bytes or source-registry revision; that activity is strictly after the frozen post-close cut, is not a stale-copy condition, and is never compared with or added to the closed v3 evidence. The commit path still revalidates the immutable exclusive-source-access capability, provisional reservation, destination, deadline, and canonical locks, but it must not demand current source-byte/time equality or a still-current post-close source-registry revision.

After every non-null init, success or failure follows one ownership order. An exact terminal-token-bearing `SQLITE_DONE` whose immediately following public remaining/pagecount reads match the token first causes the controller to request `freeze_install_v1`; failure to install and acknowledge that canonical successor is failure, while every other copy failure proceeds directly toward fencing. No freeze request precedes those counter reads. First it calls the sole `sqlite3_backup_finish`, captures the exact unmasked result, and clears `backup_ptr|finish_owed`; an `SQLITE_OK` finish after BUSY/LOCKED, drift, cancellation, deadline, incomplete copy, missing terminal token, counter mismatch, or missing freeze receipt never converts failure to success. Second, while both SQLite handles are live, it reads back `NO_CKPT_ON_CLOSE=1` on each and repeats both actual-main-handle identities. Third it calls exact nondeferred `sqlite3_close(destination)`; on the success path it then waits under the immutable deadline for every writer event admitted before the freeze to install a clean, independently valid v2 receipt, rejecting an abort or bounded-wait failure, and only then calls exact nondeferred `sqlite3_close(source)`. On other paths it closes source in the same destination-then-source order before fencing. `sqlite3_close_v2` is forbidden. A non-`SQLITE_OK` close leaves that handle owned, requests fencing/reaping, and cannot proceed as success. Immediately after successful source close and while `writer_begin_freeze_state=postterminal_v1`, the supervisor takes the exact zero-active post-close registry snapshot and source manifest. Fourth it finalizes both connection-local VFS traces and the semantic transcript, runs the independent replay, and constructs complete v3 audit bytes; it requires source zero mutation, zero denied-operation count, zero denied-mutation subset count, zero source WAL-index store attempts, exact destination allowlisted mutations, all four initial/preclose no-checkpoint readbacks, exact role/reservation/generation attribution, complete clean writer receipts/publications between the two cuts, the terminal token, the freeze-install receipt, and the final companion inventories. Fifth, while retained leaf FDs and broker capabilities remain active, it revalidates both descriptors and broker-held directory leaves, fsyncs/revalidates the retained destination FD, proves destination journal/WAL/SHM/temp/alias absence, asks the broker to fsync/revalidate the staging parent, revalidates only the retained source identity/namespace/type/link/owner/mode plus registry/access capability against the already frozen post-close manifest, then closes retained destination/source FDs and releases the broker capabilities. The postterminal freeze remains installed until the exact provisional successor and final complete revalidation permit the ready release, or until authoritative fencing permits the fenced release.

Only a prior exact `SQLITE_DONE`, its independently reproduced `terminal_copy_snapshot_sha256`, matching immediate public counters, exact freeze-install receipt, pre-finish zero-remaining relation, exact `SQLITE_OK` finish, both exact close results, both zero-active attribution cuts, successful semantic/header-publication replay, and every v3 audit/identity/fsync/absence/deadline check can produce `copy_vfs_audit_sha256`. With the same `backup_copy_source_v1` generation still active, canonical opener locks held, and `writer_begin_freeze_state=postterminal_v1`, the worker sends the complete bounded v3 audit bytes and terminal/freeze-install preimages to the supervisor. Only the supervisor's independent reproduction and acceptance may install the complete `(active,copying,provisional,g,active,backup_copy_source_v1)` successor carrying that digest. The worker obtains that exact canonical snapshot and, while the freeze and canonical locks remain held, performs the final complete predecessor, destination, capability, cancellation, deadline, two-headroom, and evidence revalidation. It then requests the ready `freeze_release_v1`; the acknowledged immutable release receipt is mandatory input to `copy_commit_v1`. A registered writer admitted after that release belongs only to a later source interval and cannot modify the closed destination or v3 audit.

Any failure before ready release first requests authoritative fencing. After that rename is authoritative, the current/replacement source-registry supervisor—not the failed producer—uses `supervisor_recovery_v1` to install the fenced freeze release from the exact fence lineage. Raw destruction may advance through `destroyed` before that content-free release, but reservation pruning is forbidden until release is authoritative. A crash after the fence rename, after destroy, or at any fenced-release sibling/rename/ack boundary is reconciled from the detailed reservation plus immutable source-registry request/evidence receipt; it never refences, depends on the dead producer, or waits until reboot for capacity. Any failure or crash after ready release but before committed rename leaves an unfrozen active provisional record that may advance only through `fencing -> destroyed`; it can never sanitize. A stale or ambiguous freeze response is reconciled by transition ID/request digest and current canonical receipt rather than retried with a new ID. No failure exposes a handle or path.

Only after every fallible copy check, close, semantic replay, v3 audit, fsync, provisional install, final revalidation, and ready freeze release succeeds does the worker request the complete `(active,sanitizing,committed,g,idle,null-writer-fields)` successor from the sole supervisor. Its `copy_commit_v1` predecessor evidence binds the exact provisional record/v3 digest/terminal token, the immutable ready-release transition receipt, and the final revalidation digest; the successor stores the release-receipt digest and final-revalidation digest in `copy_freeze_release_receipt_sha256|copy_final_revalidation_sha256` in addition to preserving `copy_vfs_audit_sha256`. The supervisor admits the mutation only with checked `effective_now + reservation_watchdog_takeover_headroom_ms + reservation_rename_headroom_ms < copy_finish_by`, held canonical opener locks, unchanged exclusive-source-access capability, and exact destination/provisional evidence; a later registered source event or registry revision is not a mismatch. It writes and fsyncs that sibling, revalidates the exact provisional record revision/hash, generation, v3 audit digest, capability, phase, release receipt, canonical-lock proof, and authenticated watchdog guard, then performs its final same-boot effective-time sample immediately before rename and requires checked `effective_now + reservation_rename_headroom_ms < copy_finish_by`. It then performs its one same-directory rename inside the rename bound. That rename is the sole copy-success linearization point; under the declared same-boot tmpfs/reboot-loss model no required fsync, allocation, deadline sample, or fallible success gate follows it. A stale/failed request, missing/wrong release receipt, failed takeover-completion guard, lost canonical lock, capability drift, or failed/equal headroom predicate requests fencing/destruction. Response loss reconciles the canonical committed successor without replaying the transition or release. Canonical locks may then release, and later constructors require its exact committed marker, v3 audit digest, ready-release receipt digest, and final-revalidation digest. Writer generations increase monotonically, never wrap or repeat, and a later raw-stage role may bind only from a valid supervisor-proven idle predecessor.

Immediately after copy closure and before planning, at most one optional base-synchronization `ledger_import_v1` opener may import only the exact contiguous suffix from the clone's copied head through the frozen plan base head, commit, and close; it is omitted only when those heads already match. The next opener is exclusively `backup_plan_v1`. Once that role commits the immutable scrub application, `ledger_import_v1` is permanently forbidden for the reservation. Only `backup_scrub_v1` may then sanitize/VACUUM/check, and it never imports ledger rows. The sole permitted later projection import is the exact contiguous resulting suffix performed by `backup_attestation_v1` as the first statements of the final transaction in §6.3.2, after which that same role may perform the final attestation write. Only `immutable_verify_v1` may perform detached post-finalization verification, and it never imports ledger rows. Each constructor and clean close follows the exact phase/marker/generation/predecessor table in §6.3.2; none may invoke online backup. There is no generic destination connection or hidden stock better-sqlite3 `backup()` path.

The dedicated `ledger_import_v1|backup_plan_v1|backup_attestation_v1` roles alone may make the one native transition `trusted 0 -> 1`, and only after verifying the exact second-layer schema/trigger/scalar/runtime hashes, selecting their precompiled policy, and proving no unreviewed statement has prepared. They execute only the manifest-bound immediate transaction, transition `trusted 1 -> 0`, verify readback, and destroy the connection after commit or rollback. Defensive mode and load-extension denial never change. Their authorizers return only `SQLITE_OK|SQLITE_DENY`, permit a stateful function only from its exact source trigger, and deny top-level use, every other trigger/view, schema mutation, savepoints, attach/detach, load-extension, and unallowlisted DML. Trusted schema is never a vec0 workaround or broad fallback.

The closed native surface therefore consists only of the immutable role constructor/readback, lossless signed-int64/BLOB results for the precompiled scalar registry, connection-local `armLedgerRecordVerifier(context)`, `armExternalEvidenceVerifier(context)`, `verifierState()`, and `clearVerifierContext(reason)`. Each verifier context binds the actual `sqlite3*`, immutable role, canonical database name, transaction-generation nonce, schema descriptor/hash, database device/inode, exact prepared SQL-text hash, parameter names/order, typed bound-value manifest, expected source trigger, verifier kind/subject/digest, and canonical raw evidence. Native trace/commit/rollback hooks clear it on first verifier invocation whether successful or not, unexpected statement, statement error, commit, rollback, savepoint activity, automatic-reprepare mismatch, host exception, or close. No SQL function, PRAGMA, table, attached schema, serialized token, or JavaScript callback can arm or copy a context.

Attestation has two non-interchangeable layers so review order stays acyclic. Before DDL exists, every release target produces `native_authority_runtime_attestation_v1`, freezing both fork package/version/source commits; the better-sqlite3 npm/source-zip/download-script/options/stock-negative/generated-amalgamation chain; upstream/raw-source, repository-patch, and patched-source hashes; build flags; direct vec-init wrapper; checked innocuous path and restricted-module implementation; every built `.node` SHA-256; SQLite version/source ID/generated-amalgamation/compile options; Node ABI; N-API version; OS/architecture; and immutable role-engine/policy-enum hashes.

The same first layer freezes the literal `ai_brain_sqlite3_open_v2_private_vfs_v1` and `ai_brain_apply_pre_btree_role_v1` implementations; the separate connection/main-VFS flag words, exact mask, stock-combined-word bypass, and requested/returned main-`xOpen` assertions; `pre_btree_role_configured_v1` order-marker implementation and results; process-global automatic-extension suppression branch and canary result; frozen ordered built-in/direct initializer symbol/hash/return-code registry; distinct source/destination connection-local VFS implementations; literal complete non-file/file/I/O/file-control matrix hash; generated pinned call-set/result hash, including the exact one-call/nonretaining `BUSYHANDLER|PDB` hints; v2 operation/payload/output-state registry; independent argument/result encoder hash, including read/write buffer digests and initialized-error outputs; exact retained leaf-FD/broker-directory-capability/fixed-basename bindings; conservative any-journal preflight; the core source-role fork before zero-page `RESERVED_LOCK`, delete suppression, and unreliable-SHM heap-index fallback suppression; separate WAL-`SQLITE_CANTOPEN` and SHM-`SQLITE_READONLY_CANTINIT` rules; WAL downgrade and SHM-read-only/no-create/no-chown/no-writable-map rules; exact Wal/WalIndex ABI, constant, compile-branch, atomic-expansion, and semantic-site registries; the local-copy refactors used jointly by SQLite and the `wal_read_attempt_v1|wal_read_epoch_v1|wal_frame_lookup_v1|backup_restart_v1` producers; their global transcript encoder, capacity, overflow behavior, and independently implemented validator hash; the complete `writer_wal_header_publish_v1` site/reason/entry/chain registry; accepted-header/publication matching; the terminal-token producer site; kernel-enforced exclusive-source-access capability; registered-writer complete header/freeze-state/event/request/transition-receipt/v2-audit-receipt/snapshot-chain encoders plus literal writer matrix/call-set; exact `freeze_install_v1|freeze_release_v1` request/evidence/receipt shapes, begin-denial edge, owner/lifetime, and restart reconciliation; both quiescent attribution cuts and source-registry restart/reconciliation protocol; `source_surface_drift|unattributed_source_mutation` precedence; v2 VFS traces; and the complete v3 copy-audit digest/receipt encoders and supervisor validator.

The first layer additionally freezes the source-registry `request_actor_role` enum and complete actor-by-edge/reason/capability matrix; the producer-owned ready-release versus supervisor-recovery-owned fenced-release split; active/fencing/destroyed postterminal recovery pairings; the reservation-prune/source-freeze interlock; `SOURCE_WRITER_REGISTRY_MAX_SOURCE_COUNT_V1`; `SOURCE_WRITER_REGISTRY_MAX_RECORD_BYTES_V1`; every request/evidence/audit byte maximum; the independently generated `source-writer-terminal-closure-envelope-v1` state graph/calculator; the content-free capacity-ledger reconstruction; and the dedicated tmpfs byte/inode terminal-reserve floor. A target mismatch, an admission that does not reserve the maximum mandatory successors, a terminal edge that can fail for capacity, or a prune that can erase a referenced fence lineage fails first-layer capability before a writable descriptor, freeze installation, destination byte, or readiness.

It additionally binds each role's exact connection flags, exact separately masked main-VFS request flags, exact returned actual-main flags, and readback; both handles' pre-Btree `NO_CKPT_ON_CLOSE=1` marker plus initial and preclose readbacks; internal-only native destination role; target-specific `AI_BRAIN_FCNTL_FILE_IDENTITY_V1` actual-VFS-handle and fixed-leaf broker adapters; `BACKUP_COPY_STEP_PAGES_V1=128`; actual null `db->busyHandler`, zero invocation count, and zero VFS lock-timeout seal despite the nonretaining Pager hint; single-native-executor init/step/immediate-counter/finish/close/audit ownership; counter equality before freeze IPC; exactly-one-finish and destination-then-source nondeferred-close order; checked effective-wall-to-boottime retry conversion; actual-handle/retained-FD/companion-manifest checks; complete semantic event/cross-link coverage, exact transcript and writer-receipt capacities, independent replay, installed-freeze receipt, both attribution cuts, and terminal clone authority; provisional/final-revalidation/ready-release/committed copy ordering, the longer canonical-opener-lock lifetime, post-release later-source exclusion, and exact deadline/fsync/complete-sibling/rename rules.

The reservation/supervisor portion freezes both complete physical record encoders and maximum lengths; the pruned-reservation tombstone encoder/domain plus its post-prune retry contract; both non-prunable transition-receipt encoders and successor-core domains; the closed actor-by-edge and actor-by-fence-reason matrices; transition request encoder/null-shape rules; sole-supervisor OFD-lease and external-manager pidfd kill/reap implementation; distinct watchdog-takeover, validation-to-rename, raw-destroy-physical-closure, and end-to-end raw-destroy measurements/formulas/enforcement; the `2*W+C+2*R` composed bound and derived destruction margin; per-mutation watchdog takeover-completion guards and nonrecursive replacement protocol; descriptor-broker/LSM/sandbox/cgroup stable-opener capability; broker revocation, tracked-cgroup kill/reap, stable two-scan and unlink protocol; reservation/source-registry crash and restart reconciliation; exact session/cgroup persistence; supervisor isolation/private raw-destruction lane; supported-target killability gate; and the complete post-copy phase/generation/import-role/lock-subset state table and wait-for graph. It also binds the defensive/trusted/load-extension seal, six-scalar names/arities/flags/domain registry, stateful context implementation, packaged wrapper, and conformance-fixture hashes. It deliberately contains no claimed S28 schema, trigger, shadow object, prepared statement, or instantiated source-trigger matrix and cannot authorize S28 SQL.

Only after exact migration DDL is authored does each target produce `schema_028_connection_capability_attestation_v1`. That second attestation hashes the target's exact first layer and additionally freezes the complete normalized S28 schema/trigger descriptor, exact `CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE main.chunks_vec USING vec0(embedding float[768])`, every vec0 shadow object/SQL/hash, vector-first guard names/SQL, instantiated role/authorizer action/source-trigger matrices, function-kind/trigger mapping, migration/scrub/verifier prepared-SQL hashes, parameter order, and typed manifest registries. For a database already proven S28, launch order is fixed: verify first-layer files before open; enter the role-specific constructor, which for a copy role installs its dbconfigs/role/VFS/token before Btree/Pager and for every other role applies its attested closed initializer; complete the frozen initializer order and readbacks; seal role/registry/authorizer/VFS; verify first-layer runtime; inspect the raw complete schema without invoking schema code; verify the second layer; only then prepare an S28 statement.

The 027→028 edge uses a third, one-way bridge rather than pretending an S27 database already has the S28 second layer. After authored DDL, exact normalized expected S28 schema, disposable exact-S27 migration rehearsal, and the second layer exist, each target produces `schema_027_to_028_transition_capability_attestation_v1`. Its §6.12 framed payload has this fixed order:

```text
contract,target_triple,
native_authority_runtime_attestation_sha256,
accepted_s27_schema_descriptor_sha256,
accepted_s27_migration_ledger_frontier_sha256,
migration_028_filename,migration_028_source_sha256,
s27_preflight_statement_manifest_sha256,
pre_ddl_scalar_probe_manifest_sha256,
migration_028_ordered_statement_manifest_sha256,
migration_028_authorizer_action_manifest_sha256,
migration_028_typed_bound_value_manifest_sha256,
expected_post_migration_ledger_sha256,
expected_s28_schema_descriptor_sha256,
schema_028_connection_capability_attestation_sha256,
containment_capability_latch_sha256
```

`contract` is exact `schema-027-to-028-transition-capability-attestation-v1`; `target_triple` fixes the packaged OS, architecture, Node ABI, and native artifact target; every digest is raw32. `accepted_s27_migration_ledger_frontier_sha256` covers the complete ordered filename/source-hash ledger through 027, including the normalized legacy-ledger form, not merely its last row. The preflight manifest contains only the exact S27 read statements and content-free result shapes from §5; the scalar manifest binds all pre-DDL probes. The ordered migration manifest covers transaction control, every prepare/bind/step/finalize action, exact SQL-text hash, parameter name/order/storage class, collision check, DDL/DML statement, and the final 028 ledger row; the authorizer manifest covers every permitted SQLite action and source object; and the typed-bound-value manifest binds every runtime-derived clock, epoch, ID, hash, and count to its reviewed derivation/range rather than permitting caller-selected values. `containment_capability_latch_sha256` is the exact already execution-proven S27/S28 latch under the migration lock. The transition-attestation digest uses §6.12 domain `schema-027-to-028-transition-capability-attestation-v1` over those fields in order. The S28 second layer never hashes this transition artifact back, so the dependency is acyclic: first layer and authored migration → expected S28/second layer → transition attestation.

Migration launch verifies the exact first layer, transition artifact, second layer, migration source file, target, and containment latch **before** opening a database. The constructor then completes and seals the first-layer initializer/role/authorizer without preparing caller or schema SQL and uses the attested raw-schema inspector, which cannot invoke a view, trigger, virtual table, or schema function, to select exactly one of these paths:

1. **Exact S27 preflight.** Raw schema must equal `accepted_s27_schema_descriptor_sha256`. A read-only preflight open selects the transition capability's preflight submanifest, verifies the complete ledger frontier and scalar probes, emits only the approved content-free results, prepares no DDL, and closes. It cannot switch to apply mode on that handle.
2. **Exact S27 apply.** Under the already-held global migration lock, a fresh transition-capability handle opens `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, then re-inspects the raw S27 descriptor and re-reads the complete ledger frontier inside that transaction before any migration statement. It reruns the bound freshness/collision/preflight predicates, then prepares and steps each exact transition-manifest entry once in order. Before commit, the raw inspector must reproduce the expected complete S28 descriptor, the exact post-migration ledger digest, every expected vec0 shadow/trigger object, and the target's `schema_028_connection_capability_attestation_v1`; no ordinary S28 statement is prepared on the transition handle. Any mismatch rolls back and destroys the handle. Only then may the exact commit occur, after which the handle closes and a fresh S28-capability connection must reopen, raw-inspect S28, verify the second layer, and verify the exact 028 ledger row before readiness.
3. **Exact S28 no-op.** Raw schema must equal the second-layer descriptor. The constructor verifies `schema_028_connection_capability_attestation_v1` before preparing the one exact second-layer ledger verification statement; an exact 028 filename/source-hash row returns no-op. A missing, conflicting, or partial row is containment failure, never repair.

If a concurrent migrator changes S27 to S28 before the apply handle obtains `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, the losing handle's mandatory in-transaction S27 reinspection fails; it rolls back, closes, and restarts only through the exact S28 no-op path. It never changes capability branch on the existing handle. An older, newer, partial, mixed, or unrecognized schema/ledger combination has no path. A crash before commit leaves SQLite's exact S27 transaction rollback and unchanged ledger; a durable commit followed by process or response loss is reconciled only by the S28 no-op path. Faults between any DDL action, post-state inspection, commit, close, or reopen leave the prepositioned containment units active and application readiness down. There is no downgrade, guessed frontier, per-statement fallback, broad trusted-schema mode, or use of the S28 attestation to authorize an S27 prepare.

Migration, maintenance, backup, restore, containment, diagnostics, and every packaged OS/architecture target import this same wrapper and require the applicable attestation. Stock better-sqlite3/sqlite3/sqlite-vec; the stock instead of option-generated SQLite amalgamation; a missing/wrong patch, private VFS, no-checkpoint seal, VFS audit, supervisor protocol, or native artifact; a globally registered/name-selectable wrapper; a loadable vec artifact; an independently exposed/unsealed or out-of-role handle; a copy destination not created and consumed by the exact native pair; an unrestricted constructor; unsupported raw-stage containment; or a first-layer, transition-layer, or second-layer mismatch fences readiness, listener, capture, processing, backup, and restore while prepositioned containment remains active. Both source forks, every target's first-layer attestation, and the native capability tests require a separate zero-P0/P1 implementation review before migration 028 DDL may be authored. The transition and schema-bound second layer are mandatory evidence for the later migration review and must exist before that DDL is prepared or run against any database.

Two additional SQL-visible functions are stateful verifier bridges and are deliberately **not** deterministic or innocuous. `retired_ledger_record_verified_v1` has exactly the seventeen arguments named in §6.3.1: `record_sha256` followed by the sixteen raw-record-derived projection fields from `sequence` through `retired_at`, excluding local `imported_at`. `external_evidence_verified_v1(kind,subject_id,evidence_sha256)` has exactly three arguments and accepts only `kind IN ('backup_plan_input_v1','backup_pre_finalization_checks_v1')`. Each returns SQLite `INTEGER 1` only when one matching host-armed context passes; every unarmed, wrong-storage-class, wrong-kind, wrong-subject, wrong-digest, wrong-field, wrong-statement, replay, or cross-connection call returns integer `0` without exposing raw evidence, key material, paths, or exception text. The ledger function parses the exact length-prefixed `R`, verifies record hash, entry hash, HMAC, chain position, and all sixteen decoded values in constant-time where applicable. For `backup_plan_input_v1`, the backup bridge verifies the closed input-file device/inode/hash, frozen base-head/plan facts, and exact prepared application bindings; for `backup_pre_finalization_checks_v1`, it verifies the exact closed pre-finalization file/device/inode, persisted check/page/scan/head facts, evidence digest, and exact prepared attestation bindings. The named SQL guards independently enforce every database-visible FK, tuple, aggregate, clock, and state equality.

Arming exists only as a host-language API; no SQL function, table, PRAGMA, trigger, or attached schema can arm it. The owning ledger importer or backup scrubber first verifies the raw evidence, opens `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` with no active savepoint, and supplies one connection-local context bound to the exact database handle, transaction-generation nonce, schema descriptor/hash, verifier kind, subject, evidence digest, canonical raw bytes/facts, exact next verifier-bearing prepared-statement hash and bound-value manifest, and exact expected trigger name. A plan-input backup context additionally binds the just-closed/reopened database device/inode, the pre-plan closed-file hash, frozen base head, and complete planned-row/action/application manifest; before its application statement, only the exact manifest-listed planned/action insert statements may execute, and any other statement clears the context. A pre-finalization backup context binds the checked database device/inode and every persisted pre-finalization file/size/page/freelist/check/scan/head fact and permits no intervening statement; ledger context retains the exact `R` and likewise permits no intervening statement. Only one context may exist. The first verifier invocation consumes it whether comparison succeeds or fails; statement error, commit, rollback, rollback-to/release of any savepoint, automatic reprepare mismatch, connection close, or host exception also clears it. Rollback/retry requires complete reverification and a fresh context. Multi-row, re-entrant, nested-trigger, cross-transaction, cross-kind, and cross-connection use therefore fails closed.

Every role registers the two stateful entrypoints in their unarmed state. Every non-verifier role permanently keeps `SQLITE_DBCONFIG_TRUSTED_SCHEMA=0`/`PRAGMA trusted_schema=OFF`; an unarmed top-level probe returns integer `0`, and schema code cannot invoke either stateful function. The two functions are registered as `SQLITE_UTF8` only—never `SQLITE_DETERMINISTIC`, `SQLITE_INNOCUOUS`, or `SQLITE_DIRECTONLY`, because direct-only would make their required trigger invocation impossible. A verifier-role authorizer's `SQLITE_FUNCTION` decision uses only the function name and SQLite-reported source trigger: it permits `retired_ledger_record_verified_v1` only from `youtube_retired_item_ledger_entries_verify_insert_v1`, and permits `external_evidence_verified_v1` only from `brain_sensitive_backup_scrub_applications_verify_insert_v1` or `brain_sensitive_backup_scrub_attestations_verify_insert_v1`. The exact schema-hashed trigger SQL supplies the respective literal `backup_plan_input_v1` or `backup_pre_finalization_checks_v1`, and the function independently requires that kind to match its armed context; no authorizer argument introspection is assumed. The authorizer denies either function at top level or from every other trigger/view/schema object and remains installed through `sqlite3_step` and automatic reprepare. Trusted schema returns off and the connection is destroyed after the one transaction. Registry/capability hashes attest role, function arity/flags, accepted kind-to-trigger mapping, trigger names/SQL, authorizer rules, host verifier implementation, and unarmed behavior.

Fingerprints and multi-field canonical context hashes named in this section use SHA-256 over UTF-8 domain bytes, one `0x00`, then the following closed frame registry. Every frame is `marker || u64be(payload_length) || payload`; the marker byte is not included in the length, and null itself still has the eight-byte zero length.

| Value class | Marker | Length and payload |
|---|---:|---|
| null | `0x00` | length `0`; no payload |
| exact UTF-8 text, including canonical ASCII enum/version/origin/ID tokens | `0x01` | exact UTF-8 byte length and bytes |
| raw bytes or decoded digest | `0x02` | exact byte length and bytes; every stored lowercase 64-hex digest is decoded to exactly 32 bytes |
| signed integer | `0x03` | length `8`; two's-complement signed-int64 big-endian |
| Boolean | `0x04` | length `1`; payload `0x00` for false or `0x01` for true |

No other marker, implicit array/object, float, exponent, decimal string, locale formatting, or unframed field is valid. Callers expand composite values into their specified ordered fields. Every clock/count/ordinal first proves exact signed-int64 range. JavaScript implementations use `bigint` or an equivalently lossless int64 representation through encoding and SQLite binding; they never pass a value outside `Number.isSafeInteger` through `number`, and vectors at `2^53-1`, `2^53`, and `2^63-1` detect rounding. Opaque text IDs use exact UTF-8 bytes. Strings are NFC-normalized only where the named input contract says so. Null and empty text are distinct.

The literal single-frame vectors are:

```text
null                  000000000000000000
empty text            010000000000000000
text "A"              01000000000000000141
raw bytes 00 01 02    020000000000000003000102
signed int64 -1       030000000000000008ffffffffffffffff
signed int64 0        0300000000000000080000000000000000
Boolean false         04000000000000000100
Boolean true          04000000000000000101
```

The framing self-test preimage is domain `typed-framing-self-test-v1`, `0x00`, then frames for null, empty text, text `A`, raw bytes `00 01 02`, signed integer `-1`, false, and true. Its exact preimage hex is `74797065642d6672616d696e672d73656c662d746573742d76310000000000000000000001000000000000000001000000000000000141020000000000000003000102030000000000000008ffffffffffffffff0400000000000000010004000000000000000101` and SHA-256 is `46cf92b834d2f39636856770238f2628ba9c999b1b52cc29b7f0c6097b0ab553`.

The static Stage 2 conformance fixture is a cryptographic encoder fixture, not a schema-028 database image, production schema descriptor, sanitized-backup publication proof, or proof that every S28 persistence surface has been scanned. It freezes the fixture-only descriptor `stage2-conformance-encoder-subset-v1`. That subset's complete ordered root-table registry is `items,transcript_sources`; its exact column descriptors are `items` primary key `(id)` with stored columns `(id,body,summary)` and `transcript_sources` primary key `(id)` with stored columns `(id,item_id,text_sha256)`. Its complete ordered survivor-table registry is `capture_metadata_cache,collections`; its rows use the exact typed primary-key/row descriptors emitted in the fixture. Its complete ordered zero-scan surface registry is `main_pages,wal`. Each registry is ordered by raw ASCII token bytes. “Complete” in fixture assertions means complete only for this named encoder subset, and a missing, extra, duplicate, or reordered token fails that fixture.

The fixture subset can never satisfy `PACKAGED_YOUTUBE_BROWSER_SCHEMA_CONTRACT`, backup publication, restore acceptance, or production descriptor-completeness gates. The production root/control table registry, complete column descriptors, row selectors, virtual/shadow-table rules, zero-scan surface registry, external-surface tokens, and byte-measurement algorithms remain intentionally unfrozen until the exact migration SQL and packaged external-surface implementation freeze. Before `PACKAGED_YOUTUBE_BROWSER_SCHEMA_CONTRACT` becomes non-null, a separate real-SQL conformance test must create exact S28 under the pinned native runtime, materialize a complete valid restricted-item graph only through packaged writers, derive the production registries from the frozen descriptor, execute all three scan phases, and reproduce every production hash through an independent encoder. The fixture-only subset is not evidence for that gate.

The normative static fixture is `implementation/fixtures/stage2-schema-conformance-vectors.json`, SHA-256 `45c922536cab353207ee5a456266e8857df07c2ecf67982b1fd66dbfe34d3d28` (5,480 lines, 434,075 bytes). It contains the literal frame/int64/clock vectors; browser-authority previous/current epoch IDs, canonical token envelope, all three domain-separated secret hashes, and distinct concrete malformed/missing/retired-epoch negatives; fixed signer-provisioning inputs plus exact receipt preimage/digest and concrete lineage mutations; fixed 32-byte ledger test key; genesis and two complete chained record bytes with expected tuple/entry/HMAC/record hashes plus concrete negative ledger records; the named fixture-only descriptor registries; structured typed primary-key/stored-column inputs plus PK/row/root/survivor preimages; structured typed rows, machine-readable sort contracts, and preimages for all four empty and two-row backup aggregates; an explicit provisioning-bound scrub-application/plan preimage; independently phase-bound scrub/pre/post zero scans and pre-finalization evidence; and a semantically matching backup-key ID/provisioning receipt plus the complete real-key-order detached sans-HMAC/final bytes. Its v4 negative surface is exactly 69 atomic replayable entries across 19 frozen categories in `negative_vector_registry`; every entry contains concrete baseline, mutation, oracle, expected-precedence-stage, and expected-outcome data. The ref-only `negative_cases` catalog is an exact 69-entry bijection, including six adjacent dual-fault ledger-precedence edges and all six directed scrub/pre/post phase substitutions; an outcome-only, missing, extra, duplicate, or unresolved case fails generation. Its deterministic materializer is `implementation/fixtures/generate-stage2-schema-conformance-vectors.mjs`, SHA-256 `f15fcdc3151b0a34e8693e570a3a1435bc1919305022f0634414a58ffc5d1331` (3,672 lines, 122,241 bytes). Validation must byte-compare two generator runs and generator stdout with the static fixture, resolve the complete registry/catalog bijection, and consume the static inputs/expected bytes from a separately implemented encoder. The clean-room v4 audit independently reconstructed all emitted typed/preimage digests, HMACs, ledger records and failure precedence, zero-scan phase bindings, and concrete negative mutations with P0/P1/P2 = 0/0/0; regenerating expected output with the implementation under test is not conformance evidence. Any fixture or generator hash change is a schema-contract change requiring renewed review.

Content digests are deliberately different: upload `body_sha256`, source `text_sha256`, per-segment text hashes, note hashes, and `applied_item_body_sha256` are ordinary SHA-256 over the exact contract-defined UTF-8 bytes with no domain or typed wrapper. `transcript-integrity-v1` is the explicitly domain-separated ordered segment fingerprint defined in §6.3. The 32-byte owner scope and queue claim tokens use `SHA-256(UTF8(domain) || 0x00 || raw32(value))`, with no typed length wrapper. Browser intent, inspect, and upload-grant secrets instead use `SHA-256(UTF8(exact-v2-domain) || 0x00 || raw32(authority_epoch_id) || raw32(secret))`. These exceptions make byte-content identity and secret verification unambiguous rather than silently subject to the fingerprint encoder.

Named domains include `youtube-browser-intent-secret-v2`, `youtube-browser-inspect-secret-v2`, `youtube-browser-upload-grant-secret-v2`, `youtube-browser-panel-binding-v1`, `youtube-browser-request-id-v1`, `youtube-browser-requester-origin-v1`, `youtube-browser-destination-binding-v1`, `youtube-transcript-job-claim-token-v1`, `enrichment-job-claim-token-v1`, and `embedding-job-claim-token-v1`. The strict browser token envelope is ASCII `yb2.<authority_epoch_id>.<secret_base64url>`, where the epoch is exactly 64 lowercase hex and the final component is the unpadded canonical 43-character base64url encoding of raw32; padding, alternate alphabet/case, whitespace, extra component, or noncanonical decoding fails before database lookup. Those three queue domains remain the complete schema-028 claim-token registry: each hashes exactly the fresh raw32 token for its own rebuilt table, and a token/hash/domain from one queue can never satisfy another. Adding another claim-bearing table or changing a domain is a schema-contract change, not a wildcard expansion. Panel, request-ID, requester-origin, and destination context hashes use the typed framing. The requester-origin input is lowercased canonical `chrome-extension://<approved-id>`. The destination-binding input is the compile-time HTTPS scheme/host/effective-port followed in fixed order by the grant, commit, and reconciliation route discriminators plus transfer-contract version; none may come from a request or response.

The panel’s `body_sha256` covers the byte-identical UTF-8 serialized upload body. The server streams and hashes those exact bytes, enforces 2 MiB independently of `Content-Length`, parses the strict contract, then recomputes normalized cues, cue count, canonical provider body, body text hash, and `transcript-integrity-v1`. A client hash/count never establishes source truth.

`request_fingerprint` uses domain `youtube-browser-grant-request-v1` and this exact order: intent hash, panel-binding hash, page-route class, inspect generation, request-ID hash, confirmed-body SHA-256, serialized byte length, cue count, extension/extractor versions, API/route/extractor/transfer contract versions, requester-origin hash, destination-binding hash, and return-route kind.

`authority_fingerprint` uses domain `youtube-browser-upload-authority-v1` and this exact order: intent hash, grant generation, owner hash, item ID, expected revision, video hash, page-route class, policy ID, policy expires-at, source delete-by, extension hash/version, requester-origin hash, destination-binding hash, API/route/extractor/transfer contract versions, extractor version, panel-binding hash, inspect generation, request fingerprint, confirmed-body SHA-256, serialized byte length, cue count, return-route kind, issued-at, not-before, grant expires-at, and intent expires-at. It is recomputed at issue and commit.

For an attached receipt, `commit_fingerprint` uses domain `youtube-browser-commit-v1` and this exact order: authority fingerprint, page-route class, server-normalization version, accepted revision, transcript source ID, source policy ID, source owner hash, source delete-by, server body text hash, transcript-integrity hash, accepted segment count, total characters, held enrichment generation, note action, note epoch, note generation, note hash, note AI flag, outcome, result code, HTTP status, and receipt time. Conflict receipts have no commit fingerprint because no source/note outcome was accepted, even though the bounded request body may already have been streamed and parsed; their immutable grant-copied request/body hash, byte length, and cue-count bindings remain explicit. Fingerprints supplement rather than replace the copied physical bindings.

Implementation tests freeze published golden byte vectors and digests for owner scope, every raw secret/token domain, raw content/note hashes, request-ID/origin/destination context, transcript integrity, request fingerprint, authority fingerprint with both policy clocks, the attached commit fingerprint, conflict nullness, and null-versus-empty framing. TypeScript, migration tests, and any extension fixture must consume the same vectors.

## 7. Trigger and backfill replacement

Migration 028 must drop and recreate every trigger lost or affected by rebuilt tables, including FTS, enrichment enqueue, embedding enqueue, workflow guards, taxonomy epochs, and transcript-recovery enqueue. A schema snapshot test compares the complete expected trigger/index set before accepting the migration.

Schema 028 is not ready merely because migration replay passes. B28 must use the attested embedded better-sqlite3/sqlite-vec authority fork pair through the immutable role wrapper, register/probe all six deterministic scalars plus the two permanently unarmed verifier entry points, implement the exact dedicated ledger/backup connection arming and authorizer paths, and adapt initial enrichment generation bootstrap, every chunk/vector deleter and chunk updater, `itemSemanticsChanged`, the shared `youtube_body_authority_v1` classifier consumer, transcript claim/dispatch/no-contact/rearm/cooldown paths, trigger-owned epoch/intent/inspect/grant issuance/closure, `insertTranscriptSource`, `insertTranscriptSegments`, `supersedeTranscriptSourcesForItem`, retention trusted clock/budget/admission/live references/schedule reservations/errors/survivor and copy-survivor/planned-item entries/root application/seal/retired IDs/external-ledger outbox/purge receipts, immutable two-column artifact and metadata-cache fencing, collection survivor handling, chat/NotebookLM restricted-item gates, exact Recall/Telegram tombstones, the no-durable-client-copy row/read protocol and complete aggregate/read/export inventory in §6.3.1, `public/sw.js` page/API caching and activation purge, item-page bootstrap and RSC payloads, library/search/collection/review/Ask/list serializers, every ZIP/download/print/share/clipboard/offline/outbound path, `brain-manual-notes-v2` journal, extension local storage, the manual evidence-seal insert, the sole attempt/health/job/finalization-seal service, the generic body-update receipt service, every backup/restore/startup/activation/key-provisioning path, the volatile maintenance-reservation supervisor, the prepositioned containment package, and every paste/upload/official-caption/owned-media-STT/automatic-recovery caller in the same reviewed change. The existing synchronous enrichment worker, `src/lib/queue/enrichment-batch.ts`, batch cron/submit/poll/apply paths, `src/app/api/items/[id]/enrich/route.ts`, enrichment result applier, embedding enqueue/backfills, and repair/rearm helpers must all implement the physical held-generation exclusion in §6.5. It must also replace the synthetic-attempt behavior in `recordManualTranscriptResolutionForItem`/`markTranscriptJobResolvedForItem` with the exact non-provider resolution transaction in §6.5, replace broad `retryTranscriptJobNow` with the closed rearm, retire generic `markTranscriptJobDone`, retire new `item_missing|item_upgrade_failed|transcript_unavailable|transcript_provider` persistence (including the worker's null-error fallback), adapt `ignoreTranscriptJob` to the exact non-provider-ignore shape, make an in-memory worker whose cascaded job/item is missing return no-effect, remove `item-upgrades.ts` inference from either generic user-text marker, and reject any raw `transcript_attempts` insert outside the sole finalizer through repository scan plus direct regression tests.

`src/lib/enrich/pipeline.ts`, `src/lib/queue/enrichment-batch.ts`, the direct enrich route, batch submit/poll/apply, and every future enrichment result applier must select the §5.2.1 weak/nonweak branch under their exact generation/revision/claim/batch CAS. The weak branch omits `title` from SQL entirely and discards the provider title; a repository scan and FTS-trigger counter reject `SET title=OLD.title` as a substitute. Backup/restore integration must additionally perform clone-wide grant-first mutable-authority invalidation and epoch rotation, verify/emit the two key-provisioning fields, execute the root-loss signer state machine when required, enforce volatile reservation admission/FD closure, and clean every same-item note-journal key without state filtering. The capability hashes include all of these implementation and helper hashes.

Every current body updater is closed in that same change. Public `updateItemCaptureContent` rejects an existing YouTube/YouTube Short item before mutation and repeats that rejection under its writer lock. Public `upgradeItemCaptureContent` likewise rejects existing YouTube items and loses the caller-supplied `requireNoActiveTranscriptSource` escape hatch: automatic recovery moves wholly to §8.2, while the three current generic-user-text callers move to the dedicated server-only service frozen in §§6.5/6.5.3. Existing-YouTube `/api/capture/url` `selected_text` (`browser_selected_text`/`client_dom`) is not that generic pair and is rejected with no mutation until it can enter the separately authorized browser intent/grant/source/receipt transaction; it cannot fall through the generic helper. `repairItemWithText` with `textKind='text'` rejects an existing YouTube item before mutation with stable code `youtube_text_requires_transcript_flow`; the repair UI directs that choice to the dedicated transcript paste/upload flow. The four transcript writers no longer call that public generic YouTube mutation path: an unexported source-attachment primitive is reachable only inside their complete policy/body/source/segments/seal/job transaction. The Recall importer never overwrites an existing YouTube body, including a `weak_metadata_capture`; it records stable no-change outcome `skipped_existing_youtube_requires_transcript_flow`, while non-YouTube Recall and repair behavior remains unchanged. No public helper accepts a Boolean, marker, source kind, prebuilt `CapturedContent`, body-transition/terminal-evidence ID, or caller-created “internal” token that can bypass these branches.

The combined body-update guard, deferred transition-to-evidence FKs, terminal evidence guards, and service CAS accept an existing-YouTube body change only as one of four closed transition kinds: the exact source-less generic-user-text pair with a fresh same-transaction §6.1.1 transition and §6.5.3 receipt; a manual source completed by its exact seal; a browser source completed by its exact attached receipt; or the exact automatic source/apply-receipt transaction in §8.2. Every other existing-YouTube body update, including a source-less `repairItemWithText`, Recall weak upgrade, direct `updateItemCaptureContent`, generic `upgradeItemCaptureContent`, selected-text upgrade, either marker alone, a mismatched pair, a body with no/mismatched transition or terminal evidence, or evidence created outside the owning transaction, fails closed with no body/revision/derived/job effect. A repository scan over `src/` and `scripts/` fails if another production `UPDATE items ... body` writer or an unreviewed call to those helpers appears without one of these explicit transition tests.

Every post-028 source insert supplies policy, accepted revision, integrity hash, exact segment count, and its kind-specific versioned provenance; every post-028 segment supplies constrained hashes/ranges. The four non-browser manual writers first insert the body transition, supersede the exact old unheld source when present, perform the common reset/body/revision/generation update, insert their new source with the preselected seal ID at that resulting revision, insert all segments, resolve any non-done transcript job through the preselected evidence ID, and insert the exact terminal seal last before commit, including when no transcript job exists or it is already done. This replacement preserves the old source's immutable segments/history and leaves one sealed active source; the current `deleteTranscriptSegmentsForItem` pattern is forbidden and only item-root deletion cascades old segments. The exact source-less generic-user-text service is the sole source-free §6.5 exception: it remains generic, creates only its non-transcript body transition/receipt, is allowed only with no active source/hold, and applies the exact closed job dispositions rather than stranding a pending/retryable job.

Regression tests cover each supported writer from held, pending, running, terminal, and historical-source states, including deterministic no-effect for every held-source replacement, byte-identical non-held body replacement, source supersession with retained old segments, wrong-policy/revision substitution, and full item deletion. A B27 binary is startup-incompatible with schema 028; release attestation must prove it cannot reach an insert/delete/backup/restore path that omits the new contract.

The fourth statement of `items_bootstrap_youtube_client_contract_and_transcript_v1` and every application/standalone backfill must first require the postinsert §5.2.1 class to be exactly `weak_metadata_capture` at the current revision and must additionally exclude:

- any item without the exact same-item `no_durable_client_copy_v1` row and recomputed four-component contract hash;
- `extraction_method='browser_link_only_v1'`;
- any item whose current revision has an exact `generic_user_text` body transition plus immutable `youtube_generic_body_update_receipts` row, independently of its current marker values;
- any item with an active transcript source;
- any item with an active content-processing hold;
- any item whose queue revision cannot equal the current item revision.

The combined trigger writes the current revision into the new job row and its final assertion requires exactly one v1 contract for each new YouTube/YouTube Short item, exactly one revision-1 transcript job for an eligible item, and no transcript job for an excluded one. Migration 028 drops the old split `items_enqueue_youtube_transcript_recovery` trigger and schema snapshots require it to remain absent. Pending reauthorization, claim, dispatch, explicit retry, stale rearm, returned-result classification, and automatic apply repeat the exact v1 client-contract/classifier requirements plus all six exclusions under their writer lock, including the current generic receipt; enqueue safety is not delegated to mutable extraction markers. A copied pending/retryable job for a `legacy_unproven` item is excluded before scheduling and remains byte-identical/inert. Link-only behavior is still not implemented or released by this migration; the exclusion only freezes its future safety prerequisite.

The enrichment and embedding enqueue triggers likewise write the exact current revision. Enrichment finalization cannot change a weak body's renderer metadata: it applies the exact omit-title branch, leaves body/revision/class byte-identical, and completes only its current generation/job CAS. No trigger releases a hold or creates browser authority.

## 8. Atomic service transaction contracts

### 8.1 Future browser commit transaction

When later feature implementation is separately authorized, the route first performs feature/auth/origin/destination/state/time gates and, **before reading one request-body byte**, requires a canonical v2 token envelope whose epoch equals the current singleton, exact epoch-bound intent/grant hashes and copied epoch, `items.content_revision=intent.expected_content_revision`, the exact current `weak_metadata_capture` class, and the same-item `no_durable_client_copy_v1` row/hash under its read lock. An old/malformed epoch performs no database lookup beyond the singleton and reads no body. Only then may it perform bounded streaming, strict parsing, and server recomputation without database mutation. It then opens one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction and must:

1. resolve idempotency by the exact intent/request/body fingerprint and immediately return any already-committed attached or conflict receipt; otherwise obtain/advance the fresh retention clock **after** bounded streaming/parsing and immediately before any CAS or mutation. Freshly reselect the authority-epoch singleton and require exact envelope/intent/grant equality, then revalidate authenticated session/owner, browser feature and every capture kill switch, schema-ready runtime mode, non-production deployment equality to the intent policy, external live-lab authority when environment is `lab` (or the exact synthetic-fixture-only predicate for `test|development`), the exact upload grant, item/video binding, immutable page-route class plus a fresh server/worker parse of the active document, extension, panel, allowed policies with `blocked_reason IS NULL`, exact `no_durable_client_copy_v1` row/hash, every copied binding/fingerprint, absence of another receipt, `items.content_revision >= expected_content_revision`, `now < grant_expires_at <= intent_expires_at <= policy_expires_at <= retention_cleanup_start_by < source_delete_by`, and `now <` every retained source-history cleanup start, with no purge phase/error/clock latch. The expected revision remains an immutable grant binding but is not required to equal the current revision at this post-stream gate. Only `standard_watch_v1` may pass in live lab; `shorts_fixture_v1` requires the packaged no-network test/development fixture; Shorts/embed/mobile/live/alternate route equality fails. A lower revision, epoch drift, equality at a cleanup-start boundary, any closer historical start, or any auth/runtime/feature/client-contract/route denial rejects with zero mutation/receipt/grant consumption and does not lazily expire authority;
2. select the one §5.2.1 classifier row under the writer lock. At `items.content_revision=expected_content_revision`, only `weak_metadata_capture` is `attached`; any other class is revision-discipline containment with no consumption/receipt. At a greater revision, `active_source_current` is `existing_transcript_conflict`, `current_generic_user_text` is `existing_item_text_conflict`, `source_less_strong_content` is `legacy_provenance_conflict`, and `source_less_ambiguous` is `ambiguous_provenance_conflict`; `weak_metadata_capture` at a greater revision and `invalid_current_authority_graph` at any revision are containment failures with no consumption/receipt. Every allowlisted source/provenance conflict wins immediately and skips note inspection;
3. only for the otherwise `attached` branch, resolve the optional normalized note before any source/body mutation: empty request is `none`; missing note is `created` with `include_in_ai=0`; exact existing normalized hash is note action `duplicate` without changing its AI flag; different existing note selects `existing_note_conflict`, records `note_action='none'`, and performs no note/content mutation;
4. for any allowlisted conflict, preselect one fresh receipt ID, CAS-consume the grant and terminalize the intent with that same ID, insert only the immutable conflict receipt last with the original expected revision and every accepted-source field null, change no item/source/note/derived/job row, and commit that branch;
5. for `attached`, preselect the common source/obligation ID plus receipt/transition IDs, insert the exact `browser_attachment` body transition, atomically mark the grant consumed and intent committed with that terminal receipt ID, execute the complete §6.1.2 reset/body/revision/generation update, create the policy-bound active source and ordered segments at the new revision, insert its guarded pending obligation with the exact live source/receipt/client-storage admission references, then insert the first exact schedule reservation or select-and-exact-reconcile the existing same-item reservation before any terminal evidence; the deferred source/obligation/reservation FKs remain open only until those writes;
6. create the exact held row/deadline for the newly allocated generation, which makes the rearmed enrichment singleton unclaimable;
7. if a non-done transcript job exists, perform the exact `browser_visible_transcript` non-provider resolution using the transition's preselected receipt ID under the writer lock, preserving its prior attempt/policy/revision audit fields and inserting no fabricated attempt; an absent or already-done job remains unchanged;
8. create the missing AI-off note if selected and insert the immutable attached receipt last so its terminal guard verifies the consumed grant, committed intent, and complete reset/source/hold/note/job graph before commit.

Any failure within its selected branch rolls back every branch effect, including grant consumption. A feature/kill-switch/deployment/auth flip at the deterministic barrier after body parse but before this fresh gate is a tested zero-effect denial; no pre-stream decision survives as mutation authority. A post-commit response loss returns the same receipt on reconciliation and performs no second mutation.

### 8.2 Automatic recovery apply/finalize transaction

After a provider result, the sole finalizer opens one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, reconciles an existing exact claim attempt/seal, applies the lease and generic-user-text revision classifiers, then applies the policy/deadline/runtime classifier below before inspecting content or choosing ordinary result behavior. Only an actual success for which every one of those classifiers leaves authority current enters the R-032 apply branch, which must:

1. require the exact running transcript job, claim-token hash, unexpired lease, expected revision, current item, canonical job/item/provider video identity, `youtube_body_authority_v1.authority_class='weak_metadata_capture'` at that expected current revision, the exact same-item `no_durable_client_copy_v1` row/hash, no existing recovery receipt, no active transcript source of any kind, no active hold, exact same-item job policy with `blocked_reason IS NULL`, non-deleted item, and a fresh high-water database-clock proof `now < policy_expires_at <= retention_cleanup_start_by < source_delete_by`; inside the writer lock also freshly require no retention phase/error/clock latch, the exact budget profile, deployment class `lab`, the exact non-production externally authorized lab predicate for that policy, a schema-ready standard worker plan, transcript recovery feature/worker switches still enabled, and every recovery kill switch open. Provider cooldown was already enforced at claim and immediately before contact; once a request was dispatched, a later global cooldown does not discard its returned result. No attempt row exists merely because the job was claimed;
2. validate the exact `youtube-recovery-result-v1` and canonical provenance contract, reconstruct the `youtube-timedtext-render-v1` source text and separate metadata-bearing `youtube-body-v1` item body, preselect the common source/obligation ID plus recovery-receipt/body-transition IDs, insert the exact `automatic_recovery` transition, execute the complete §6.1.2 reset/body/revision/generation update, insert the deadline-bound active source plus ordered parsed segments, insert its guarded pending obligation with the exact live source/recovery-receipt/client-storage admission references, and then insert the first exact schedule reservation or select-and-exact-reconcile the existing same-item reservation before attempt/terminal evidence, with no filesystem artifact side effect;
3. insert one terminal `success` attempt carrying a fresh finalization-seal ID, the exact pre-apply expected revision and claim-token hash, `observed_content_revision=expected_content_revision`, and `provider_result_state='success'`;
4. record the allowlisted provider success/health effect exactly once, keyed by that unique claim/attempt rather than by result-handler invocation;
5. point the job at the attempt, mark it done, and clear only that exact claim/lease using the transition's preselected recovery-receipt ID;
6. insert the immutable recovery-apply receipt last in the body-transition cycle so its terminal guard verifies the complete reset/source/segments/attempt/health/job graph;
7. insert the exact §6.5.2 finalization seal after its attempt/health/job/recovery-receipt facts are complete;
8. commit.

A stale or duplicate committed receipt makes retry/finalization content-free and no-effect. A crash before commit leaves no body mutation; a crash after commit leaves a done job plus receipt and cannot replay the provider/source apply.

Policy/deadline/runtime drift after a provider result has its own exact content-free disposition before ordinary success/retry/error branching. Under one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, reconcile an existing exact attempt first, advance the retention clock, and require the exact live claim/lease, same current expected revision, unchanged item/video with §5.2.1 class exactly `weak_metadata_capture`, exact same-item `no_durable_client_copy_v1` row/hash, no source/hold/receipt, and same-item statically allowed policy (`blocked_reason IS NULL` plus the immutable method/environment/rights/retention/profile shape, without assuming either clock is still live). After generic-user-text stale-revision classification but before any content validation/apply, select exactly one reachable code in this precedence: `source_deadline_elapsed_before_finalize` when `now >= source_delete_by`; otherwise `policy_expired_before_finalize` when `now >= policy_expires_at`; otherwise `runtime_authority_revoked_before_finalize` when the fresh deployment/external-lab/schema-ready standard-worker/feature/worker/retention/clock/budget/client-contract/kill-switch predicate in step 1 is not fully allowed. Because valid immutable policy shape requires `policy_expires_at <= retention_cleanup_start_by < source_delete_by`, the policy-expired code owns the interval from policy expiry up to but excluding delete-by—including the entire cleanup window—and the source-deadline code owns equality and later time. If none applies, normal result handling continues.

The selected boundary disposition inserts one `skipped` attempt with a fresh finalization-seal ID, the exact running policy/revision/claim tuple, `attempt_number=job.attempts`, `observed_content_revision=expected_content_revision`, the selected `result_code`, provider `youtube_innertube_timedtext`, actual normalized `provider_result_state`, `started_at=job.claimed_at`, and `finished_at=created_at=now`. A returned success stores no transcript or error/status facts; a returned retryable or terminal/manual result stores only its allowlisted normalized error code, exact stable message, and optional bounded status. The same transaction updates provider health exactly once from those actual provider-result fields, points the job to the attempt, sets `state='manual_needed'`, `completed_at=updated_at=now`, `next_run_at=NULL`, `last_provider=attempt.provider`, and the selected stable disposition code/message, clears only the exact live claim, and inserts the exact §6.5.2 seal last. Counts, ceiling, priority, policy, expected revision, creation time, and identity remain unchanged. It creates no body/source/segment/recovery-receipt/derived/queue/usage effect. Duplicate/restart delivery returns the existing exact attempt/seal/job with no second health update. At `now >= lease_expires_at`, lease sweep owns instead: the expired provider result creates no **result-bearing** attempt and has no provider-health effect, while the owning sweep may insert and seal only its exact `transcript_worker` stale-claim or generic-user-text stale-revision attempt from §6.5.

Ordinary live-authority retryable and terminal provider results use the same immediate finalizer and this closed transition table; `now` below is the one fresh database time used for attempt, health, job, and finalization seal:

| Actual normalized result | Attempt | Job transition |
|---|---|---|
| retryable, not throttled, `attempts < max_attempts` | `state='retryable_error'`, `retryable=1`, `provider_result_state='retryable_error'`, exact normalized error/message/status, `observed_content_revision=expected_content_revision`, null result code | `state='retryable_error'`; preserve attempts/ceiling/policy/revision/identity; `next_run_at = now + 1800000 * MIN(8, 2^(attempts-1))`; null completion |
| retryable, not throttled, `attempts = max_attempts` | same retryable attempt | `state='manual_needed'`; preserve exhausted ceiling; `next_run_at=NULL`, `completed_at=now`, and exact provider error/message |
| retryable and throttled by the exact registry-consistent `timedtext_http_429`/status-429 row or null-status `youtube_antibot_metadata_only` row | same retryable attempt | always `state='retryable_error'`; set `max_attempts=MAX(old.max_attempts,attempts+3)` as the explicit throttle retry-window extension; provider health sets `new_cooldown_until=MAX(COALESCE(old_cooldown_until,0),now+2700000+FLOOR(random*900000))` with injected `0 <= random < 1`; `next_run_at=MAX(base_retry_at,new_cooldown_until)`; null completion |
| terminal/manual | `state='terminal_error'`, `retryable=0`, `provider_result_state='manual_needed'`, exact normalized error/message/status, `observed_content_revision=expected_content_revision`, null result code | `state='manual_needed'`; preserve attempts/ceiling/policy/revision/identity; null schedule and `completed_at=now` |

Every row points `last_attempt_id` to the new attempt, copies its provider and stable error facts, clears `claimed_at`, `claim_token_hash`, and `lease_expires_at`, and sets `updated_at=now`. The attempt insert, actual-outcome health reduction, job transition, and §6.5.2 finalization seal are one ordered transaction; a seal cannot commit with a running/stranded job. A pre-dispatch failure instead has null provider-result/observed-revision fields, no provider-health effect, and only its separately allowlisted worker/containment transition. No path inserts an attempt or mutates provider outcome state before result finalization, and no historical null-authority attempt can satisfy these transactions.

Reconciliation first looks up the unique claim tuple. An exact attempt plus finalization seal is the terminal fact even after a later reviewed rearm; duplicate delivery, handler restart, or response/process loss returns content-free no-effect and never recomputes cooldown, extends the ceiling, or changes health again. A mismatched/partial attempt or missing seal is containment failure, not a repair. Forced failure after attempt insert, health reduction, job update, or seal insert rolls back all four plus any recovery content effects.

The generic-user-text revision-drift `skipped` result in §6.5 is a separate disposition and is not permission to weaken claim ownership. A returned provider success, retryable error, or terminal/manual result opens one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction, obtains a fresh database time, and applies this exact order:

1. Reconcile an already committed exact claim attempt first. Only an existing `skipped`/`stale_item_revision` attempt whose job is already `ignored` and points to it is a successful replay; it returns content-free no-effect and does not touch provider health again. Any other state/claim mismatch is no-effect, not a repair.
2. Otherwise require the exact running job/item, positive attempt count, same-item immutable policy, expected revision and claim-token hash supplied by the claimant, no prior attempt for that claim, no recovery receipt, and `now < lease_expires_at`. Require `items.content_revision > job.expected_content_revision`, no active source or hold, and §5.2.1 class exactly `current_generic_user_text`, including both `extraction_method='youtube_user_provided_text'` and `capture_quality='user_provided_full_text'` plus its exact `generic_user_text` body transition and bound `youtube_generic_body_update_receipts` row for the current revision/body hash. This stale-revision branch is selected before any normal content apply; marker literals without that transition/receipt are containment failure, and its retained policy row cannot reauthorize content even when expired.
3. Insert exactly one `skipped` attempt with a fresh finalization-seal ID for `attempt_number=job.attempts`, the attempted normalized provider, the job's policy/revision/claim tuple, `observed_content_revision=items.content_revision`, `result_code='stale_item_revision'`, the actual normalized `provider_result_state`, `started_at=job.claimed_at`, and `finished_at=created_at=now`; duration is therefore exact. A provider success has null error/status; a retryable or terminal/manual result stores only its allowlisted normalized error code, exact stable message, and optional bounded status. Every transcript/artifact/URL/title/body/raw-response/raw-error fact is null or absent.
4. Only after that unique insert, update provider health once in the same transaction from the attempt's persisted **actual normalized provider result**, not from `stale_item_revision`: `success` records the normal success effect; `retryable_error` or `manual_needed` records its normal allowlisted error/status effect. The health mutation is conditional on the new attempt insert, so replay cannot reset or increment health twice.
5. Point the job at that attempt and set `state='ignored'`, `completed_at=updated_at=now`, `next_run_at=NULL`, `last_provider=attempt.provider`, and the exact stale-revision code/message. Clear `claimed_at`, `claim_token_hash`, and `lease_expires_at`; preserve `attempts`, `max_attempts`, `priority`, `policy_decision_id`, `expected_content_revision`, creation time, item/video identity, and every unrelated field.
6. Insert the exact §6.5.2 finalization seal and commit with no item/source/segment/derived/enrichment/embedding/usage mutation.

At `now >= lease_expires_at`, the result handler cannot accept or persist the returned provider outcome. Equality and later time invoke the generic-user-text branch of the exact sweep in §6.5: it writes and seals one `skipped`/`stale_item_revision` attempt with the current observed revision, provider `transcript_worker`, null `provider_result_state`/error/status, transitions the job to the same `ignored` shape, and has no provider-health effect. If the result transaction wins before expiry, the sweep sees `ignored` and is no-effect; if the sweep/source attachment/item deletion wins, the result sees a changed/missing claim and is no-effect. If normal result apply wins before the generic-user-text writer, its new active source makes that later generic update reject. A forced failure after the attempt insert, job update, or seal insert rolls back the complete sweep; response/process loss after commit replays step 1.

For every post-028 actual timed-text result, the immutable attempt is the health event. The provider-health reducer is no longer callable before or outside one of these immediate finalization transactions; it accepts the same database handle, runs only when the unique attempt insert changed one row, and derives its input from that attempt's `provider_result_state`, closed-registry error, and status. The existing durable provider-health projection remains operational/global and is not FK-bound to item-root history, but all other direct setting writes/calls are retired and rejected by a repository scan. Attempt insertion, projection mutation, job transition, and finalization seal commit or roll back together. This conditional unique-insert design is the exact-once key; a separate handler-invocation flag is not authority.

## 9. Schema capability and release compatibility

`PACKAGED_YOUTUBE_BROWSER_SCHEMA_CONTRACT` remains null until SQL, tests, and review freeze the exact 028 filename and SHA-256. The final descriptor must attest:

- exact migration ledger filename and packaged source hash;
- exact `schema_027_to_028_transition_capability_attestation_v1` target/first-layer/S27-schema/full-ledger/preflight/scalar/ordered-statement/action/typed-bound-value/post-ledger/expected-S28/second-layer/containment-latch field order and digest, plus the non-fallback S27-preflight/S27-apply/S28-no-op launch state machine;
- exact columns/defaults/not-null/PK positions and complete feature-owned checks;
- exact `STRICT` status and storage-class guards for every new/rebuilt authority surface;
- exact table SQL for every 028-created or rebuilt table;
- exact normalized SQL and output-column order for `youtube_body_authority_v1`, with no second classifier view or caller-local branch table;
- exact ordered foreign keys, referenced columns, `ON UPDATE`, `ON DELETE`, match, and deferrability behavior;
- exact triggers and indexes, including every named provenance/shape/immutability/body-update/delete guard, partial predicate, and expression SQL;
- successful exact probes for all six deterministic scalars plus unarmed-zero probes for `retired_ledger_record_verified_v1` and `external_evidence_verified_v1` on every ordinary connection before any schema-028 writer is enabled; exact package/version/upstream/source-tarball/raw-source/repository-patch/patched-source/build/native-binary/generated-versus-stock-amalgamation/ABI/compile-option hashes for **both** named source forks and their sole combined `.node`; the direct embedded vec0 initializer, restricted constructor, checked innocuous `xCreate|xConnect` path, immutable connection-role engine, and internal-only online-backup destination role; the exact private-open signature and implementation, separate connection/main-VFS flag words and mask, pre-Btree role/config helper plus order marker, automatic-extension suppression branch/canary, ordered initializer registry, and both connection-local VFS implementations; literal complete non-file/file/I/O/file-control matrix hash, exact nonretaining `BUSYHANDLER|PDB` call rows, v2 trace/payload/output-state registry, independent encoder, and generated call-set/result hash; exact source/destination connection, requested-main, returned-main flags/readback, WAL-only source preflight, conservative any-journal preflight, the pre-RESERVED zero-page source fork, and separate WAL-open and SHM-map failure results;
- the exact Wal/WalIndex ABI, constants, native endian/alignment, atomic expansion, absent compile branches, and semantic capture-site registry; byte-exact `wal_read_attempt_v1|wal_read_epoch_v1|wal_frame_lookup_v1|backup_restart_v1` schemas and global transcript; retry/mapping/lock/probe/downstream-read cross-links; exact capacities and fail-on-overflow policy; separately hashed producer and independent validator; complete `writer_wal_header_publish_v1` call-reason/entry/chain registry; accepted-header/publication matching; both zero-active physical-attribution cuts; and the exact terminal-token producer and preimage;
- the kernel-enforced exclusive-source-access capability; registered-writer complete-record/freeze-state/event/transition-receipt/v2-audit-receipt/snapshot-chain/restart attribution, header-publication evidence, and unattributed-mutation failure; both copy handles' pre-Btree marker plus initial/preclose no-checkpoint-on-close readbacks; `AI_BRAIN_FCNTL_FILE_IDENTITY_V1` actual-VFS-handle and fixed-leaf broker adapters for every packaged target; `BACKUP_COPY_STEP_PAGES_V1=128`; actual null busy-handler/zero-invocation/VFS-timeout seal; init/finish/close/audit ownership; unmasked results/counters and counter-before-freeze ordering; checked effective-wall/boottime conversion; retained-FD/broker-capability/companion/two-headroom/fsync/complete-v3-audit/freeze-install/provisional/final-revalidation/ready-release/committed/complete-sibling-rename machine with canonical locks held through commit; complete post-copy role/generation/two-import-window state table; role policy/statement/action registries; dbconfig seal; and dedicated verifier arity/flags/authorizer/trigger/host-implementation hashes;
