- the exact total interprocess lock order, allowed per-path subsets, reverse-release/canonical-reacquire handoff protocol, wait-for-graph checker, same-tmpfs volatile `brain-backup-maintenance-reservation-v1` and source-writer complete physical record/transition-receipt encoders/maximums, closed actor-by-edge and actor-by-fence-reason matrices, transition request/null-shape and detailed-versus-pruned retry rules, persisted writer session/cgroup identity, sole-supervisor OFD lease plus external-manager pidfd kill/reap protocol, separate watchdog-takeover, validation-to-rename, raw-destroy-physical-closure, and end-to-end raw-destroy measurements/formulas/enforcement plus per-mutation takeover-completion guards and checked `2*W+C+2*R` composition, authenticated IPC/revision/request/role/state/crash protocol, source-admission supervisor snapshot plus reservation/staging bijection and strict `raw_destroy_by` ordering, descriptor-broker/LSM/sandbox/cgroup stable-opener capability, broker revocation and tracked-cgroup closure, canonical-lock-free fencing/destruction lane, complete stable pre/post-unlink all-process FD/device/inode/path scans, later canonical reconciliation/terminal-pruning proof, source-registry restart containment, and deadlock/timeout probes;
- absence of any raw `claim_token`, grant, or intent-secret column;
- the link-only/current-generic-receipt recovery exclusions, ordered combined insert-bootstrap trigger and allocator, weak-renderer-input update guard plus weak-enrichment title-omission branch, semantic-event/chunk/bridge immutability and pre-conflict guards, authority-epoch singleton/token binding/clone rotation, epoch-scoped permanent inspect-issuance history plus trigger-owned current-intent CAS, terminal intent/grant receipt FKs and trigger-owned nonreceipt coupling, the panel-binding one-time edge, every held-generation enrichment/item/batch/embedding denial trigger, exact 600,000-ms queue leases, trusted-time bidirectional jump latches, retention budget/admission/live-reference/schedule/error state, immutable live/copy survivor entries, exact root application/seal/retired-ID/ledger objects, two-column artifact/metadata/collection guards, no-durable-client-copy table/server/service-worker/five-state-journal/extension contract, exact copy-only application/attestation provisioning-kind and receipt fields, root-loss signer reprovisioning journal/receipt lineage, offline-clone scrub inventory, and exact `brain-sensitive-backup-scrub-v2` plus prepositioned containment runtime hashes.

The capability reader must extend its current table contract to inspect `PRAGMA foreign_key_list` plus exact normalized table SQL; columns/checks/triggers/indexes alone are insufficient.

Release tooling must treat schema 028 as unsupported by B27 unless a later independently reviewed compatibility rule proves a pristine exception. Any body transition/generic receipt, live browser source/receipt, intent/grant, hold, client-storage contract, retention obligation/reservation/survivor/root/retired-ID/ledger/attestation fact, revision-dependent in-flight job, attempt-finalization seal, recovery-apply receipt, or new literal blocks B27 activation and automatic restoration. The independently packaged S27/S28 containment units are the only pre-DDL exception and must be armed before migration. Migration 028 is not added to the audited-additive rollback allowlist merely because DDL succeeds.

Normal rollback is forward containment: keep schema 028, keep restricted features disabled, stop relevant claimant/provider workers, and deploy a schema-aware fixed binary while the prepositioned containment package and both retention units continue running. A bounded maintenance lock may pause only the ordinary unit under the exact stored-start/restart/execution proof in §6.3.2; the supervised fallback never stops. Rollback never starts B27 or leaves deadline enforcement stopped. Backup restore remains an explicit corruption-only operator decision with quantified unrelated-data-loss risk and must still pass the offline v2 quarantine/clone-scrub/attestation/high-water gates; restore never bypasses retention.

## 10. Mandatory test/evidence matrix before Stage 2 GO

### Migration and schema

- clean 001–028 replay and exact second-run no-op;
- populated 027 upgrade with every historical literal;
- legacy pre-hash `_migrations` upgrade and hash mismatch refusal;
- exact prefix-guard grandfathering and new-duplicate rejection;
- concurrent migrator serialization and exact ledger re-read inside the immediate migration transaction;
- every packaged target independently reproduces `schema_027_to_028_transition_capability_attestation_v1` and mutates each framed field: target, first-layer digest, exact S27 raw-schema descriptor, complete through-027 ledger frontier, 028 filename/source hash, S27 preflight/scalar manifest, ordered SQL/action/typed-bound-value manifests, expected post-ledger digest, expected S28 descriptor, second-layer digest, and containment latch. Each mutation, missing field, field reorder, cross-target artifact, S26/S28/partial/mixed prestate, unknown ledger row, or artifact/source disagreement fails before the first unauthorized prepare or database write;
- the S27 preflight handle is read-only and cannot select apply; the S27 apply handle obtains `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, re-inspects exact S27 and its full ledger, reruns freshness predicates, and executes every transition statement exactly once in order. Before commit it raw-inspects the exact S28 descriptor/post-ledger and verifies the second layer without preparing ordinary S28 SQL; after commit it closes and a fresh S28-capability handle repeats raw-schema/second-layer/ledger verification before readiness. The S28 path verifies the second layer before its one exact ledger no-op query. A transition handle cannot prepare an S28 ordinary statement, an S28 handle cannot authorize S27 preflight/DDL, and no handle changes capability branch or enables trusted schema;
- crash, process loss, response loss, statement error, and concurrent-migrator injection occurs before and after every transition prepare/bind/step/finalize, S27 reinspection, post-S28 inspection, ledger write, commit, close, reopen, second-layer verification, and no-op response. Every precommit fault leaves exact S27 plus the old ledger; a durable commit reconciles only as exact S28 no-op; a loser that observes S28 after obtaining the immediate lock rolls back/closes before reopening through the S28 branch; every partial schema/ledger state keeps containment active and readiness down;
- restoration of both `foreign_keys` and `legacy_alter_table` after success and every forced failure;
- row-count/stable-hash/index/trigger/FK-manifest preservation;
- exact preservation of AUTOINCREMENT `sqlite_sequence` high-water marks;
- every new ordinary table is exact `STRICT`; database checks reject every value whose post-affinity stored class or semantic shape is wrong, and preflight rejects every historical wrong stored class without a ledger row. Packaged service/JSON boundaries separately reject coercible wrong-type caller inputs before binding; tests explicitly distinguish that boundary from SQLite's documented acceptance of a coercible value whose affinity-normalized stored class/value is canonical;
- a fresh post-028 item commits at revision 1 with one enrichment job at generation 1 and stored next allocator 2; caller-supplied bootstrap variants, missing/duplicate initial jobs, and a first body transition that tries to reuse generation 1 fail. The single `items_bootstrap_youtube_client_contract_and_transcript_v1` trigger is tested with unrelated `AFTER INSERT` triggers created before and after it and with `recursive_triggers` off/on: every eligible fresh YouTube item ends with exactly one v1 client contract and one revision-1 transcript job, every excluded/non-YouTube shape has the specified zero cardinality, and forced failure after each ordered statement leaves no item, contract, transcript job, enrichment job, or FTS residue. The schema snapshot hashes the combined trigger SQL and proves the old split trigger is absent. Populated migration fixtures prove deterministic legacy enrichment generations and reject every nonterminal legacy embedding row lacking provable generation while preserving inert terminal null-generation history;
- all six deterministic scalar registrations/probes succeed natively with exact flags for every packaged application/migration/script/backup/restore/maintenance connection path and the content-hashed empty, CRLF/LF, embedded-NUL, BMP/non-BMP, non-ASCII, arbitrary `80ff`/raw32 BLOB, null-time, repeated/equal-cue, multi-segment-order, weak/strong/ambiguous body, canonical/noncanonical URL, trusted-clock, strict typed-JSON, null/empty/raw/text/Boolean, and signed-int64 boundary vectors; absent, wrong, nondeterministic, normalizing, order-insensitive, JavaScript-string-mediated, rounded-`number`, or wrongly flagged registration fails before DDL or writes, and direct SQL cannot bypass a body/evidence/clock trigger or classifier view;
- every closed connection role `ordinary_v1|migration_v1|maintenance_v1|immutable_verify_v1|ledger_import_v1|backup_copy_source_v1|backup_copy_destination_v1|backup_plan_v1|backup_attestation_v1|backup_scrub_v1` and every packaged caller/OS/architecture target reads back `defensive=1`, `trusted_schema=0`, and load-extension disabled after the ordered initializer and after every allowed transaction or copy. Copy-role tests prove `pre_btree_role_configured_v1` occurs before `sqlite3BtreeOpen`, `sqlite3PagerOpen`, `sqlite3PagerReadFileheader`, every initializer, every auto-extension callback, and every VFS call; the first fixed-main VFS call occurs only after that marker. A test-only process-global automatic-extension canary is registered and never invoked; built-ins/direct initializers occur in the frozen order; the initial and preclose `NO_CKPT_ON_CLOSE` readbacks remain true. Pinned successful opens emit exactly one `SQLITE_FCNTL_BUSYHANDLER` and one `SQLITE_FCNTL_PDB` on each main handle in their exact Btree phases, both return `SQLITE_NOTFOUND` with `denied=false`, inspect/retain zero pointer bytes, and still read back null `db->busyHandler` function/argument, zero invocation count, and zero lock timeout; a second, late, null-argument, dereferencing, retaining, or pointer-reporting hint fails. For each role, tests attempt `unsafeMode`, `loadExtension`, `setDefensive(false)`, `setTrustedSchema(true)`, SQL/host extension loading, `PRAGMA trusted_schema=ON`, `PRAGMA writable_schema`, `ATTACH`, `DETACH`, reserved function/module/role/VFS-registry replacement, named/global/URI VFS selection, unallowlisted schema/DML, multi-statement `exec`, statement-tail smuggling, and automatic reprepare after schema/policy drift; copy tests additionally attempt to clear no-checkpoint-on-close and invoke all three automatic-extension registration APIs. Every attempt hard-fails without changing any dbconfig, role, registry, VFS binding, prepared-statement manifest, initializer order, or database byte. Both non-interchangeable `migration_v1` capability branches and `backup_scrub_v1` additionally cross every permitted exact manifest entry and every one-field SQL/action/bound-value substitution; cross-branch prepare, same-handle branch switch, wrong raw-schema descriptor, and fallback all fail before statement preparation. Only the three verifier roles may perform their native `trusted 0 -> 1 -> 0` transition, and they always close after commit or rollback with defensive/load denial unchanged;
- native online-backup tests prove `backup_copy_destination_v1` is absent from the public enum and cannot be constructed, returned, pointer-recovered, or installed as a reservation role; the source role cannot prepare caller/schema SQL, mutate a source surface, install a handler, select/register/replace a VFS, or perform a second copy; and the exact pair alone copies `main -> main` into the no-follow/exclusive precreated same-tmpfs inode. Every packaged target proves the source connection word is exactly `SQLITE_OPEN_READONLY|SQLITE_OPEN_NOFOLLOW|SQLITE_OPEN_PRIVATECACHE|SQLITE_OPEN_EXRESCODE`, its distinct main-VFS word entering `sqlite3BtreeOpen` and `xOpen` is exactly `SQLITE_OPEN_READONLY|SQLITE_OPEN_MAIN_DB|SQLITE_OPEN_NOFOLLOW`, and returned actual flags are exactly `SQLITE_OPEN_READONLY|SQLITE_OPEN_MAIN_DB`; the destination proves the corresponding exact connection word `SQLITE_OPEN_READWRITE|SQLITE_OPEN_NOFOLLOW|SQLITE_OPEN_PRIVATECACHE|SQLITE_OPEN_EXRESCODE`, main-VFS word `SQLITE_OPEN_READWRITE|SQLITE_OPEN_MAIN_DB|SQLITE_OPEN_NOFOLLOW`, and returned actual word `SQLITE_OPEN_READWRITE|SQLITE_OPEN_MAIN_DB`. Tests reject every extra/missing bit, the stock combined `connection_flags|MAIN_DB` propagation, a wrong mask, `PRIVATECACHE|EXRESCODE` reaching the VFS, create/URI/shared-cache/named/caller VFS, or connection/main-word aliasing. For every normative matrix row, tests exercise the allowed shape and every one-field flag/opcode/surface/result mutation. Every `sqlite3_vfs` and `sqlite3_io_methods` entry point is classified as allowed, expected non-denied `SQLITE_NOTFOUND` internal hint/fallback, or denied; none is unclassified. The generated call-set from null-init, success, retry, fatal-step, finish-rollback, commit, and both close paths is an exact matrix subset and contains both pinned Btree hints. ### Retention, backup, restore, and startup

- browser and lab-public creation cannot commit without source-first/deferred exact obligation admission, both live source/terminal-evidence references, exact reservation insertion/reconciliation, a newly-created-under-v1 client-storage contract, fresh transition/application, and eventual receipt; standalone/extra obligation, reservation-only, source-only, receipt-only, legacy-unproven item, wrong family/cache/client scope/profile/start/deadline, unsafe artifact path, any NotebookLM request, direct live-control deletion, deadline extension, and retired item-ID reuse all fail. - profile fixtures exercise the exact 2-GiB/256-MiB/2,048-entry/50,000-survivor/64-obligation/eight-live-root ceilings; the maximum supported WAL-index region/probe workload; the exact preallocated semantic-transcript, writer-publication-receipt, and v3-audit-receipt capacities; sticky fail-on-overflow behavior; and three consecutive worst-case live, online-copy—including complete semantic capture, independent replay, both attribution cuts, terminal-token validation, and v3 finalization—sanitize, watchdog-takeover, validation-to-rename, raw-destroy-physical-closure, and full raw-destroy-sequence measurements. Every schema/producer/validator/capacity/overflow-policy or fixture-hash mutation changes the retention profile hash and denies authority. Concurrent first-item source commits with overlapping closed reservation intervals serialize so one fails; nonoverlapping slots pass, and later same-item authority must reuse the exact slot. Equality fences every reader/writer/claimant except the explicitly equality-permitted destruction predicates; measured-budget/profile/device/headroom/closure/sequence drift or overage denies authority and starts containment, and each measured phase, takeover, rename, physical closure, and complete destruction sequence finishes inside its own frozen and composed bounds;
- artifact tests cross legacy `path`, preferred `relative_path`, both-null failed/skipped, both non-null same-target, and both non-null divergent-target rows, then race insert/update/delete/UPSERT/`REPLACE`, traversal, rename, symlink, hardlink, inode/device replacement, directory removal, and power-loss/fsync faults. Every effective target must be beneath its exact canonical `<root>/<item_id>/` owner directory, and runtime-preferred target and manifest target must match. Pending rows freeze every actual 014/016 column; purge uses fd-relative no-follow operations and fsyncs every parent/root before `artifacts_purged`; restart resumes from guarded rows/manifests after any partial unlink without guessing a path;
- multiple same-item obligations share non-unique application/seal/receipt pointers and one exact typed set digest; subset, superset, cross-item, wrong raw-ID sort, wrong earliest deadline/budget/profile/cache/client scope/count/manifest/root set, partial completion tuple, receipt-only, second receipt, wrong external-ledger sequence/full tuple/hash, non-mirrored outbox, and child-unique regressions fail. - durable survivor entries cover item/citation chats/messages, exact Recall (`blocked_unknown`, zero chunks, every nullable field) and Telegram replay tombstones, enrollment survivors, zero NotebookLM, matching `capture_metadata_cache`, orphan auto-tag/AI-topic/**auto-collection** deletion, byte-identical manual/system/shared tag/topic/collection rows with exact remaining link sets, every cascade/FTS/vec shadow, and an unknown synthetic direct/indirect no-FK/`SET NULL`/cache/client table. A correct application+root-origin pending-outbox plan alone fences and resumes; missing/wrong/copy-origin outbox, seal-only, raw-delete, subset, and wrong-postimage paths fail. Faults before/after plan commit, external append, fsync, independent replication, projection import, root delete, outbox mirror, and seal prove an external retirement always precedes deletion and an orphan entry scrubs every older restore candidate; restart after root uses hashed entries to prove postimages;
- force distinct `T_plan<T_append<T_live_delete` and separate `T_clone_delete` clocks. Assert `application.created_at=seal.retirement_authorized_at=live retired_at=ledger retired_at=T_plan`, `seal/obligation/receipt db_root_purged_at=live local_root_absent_at=T_live_delete`, and copy `local_root_absent_at=T_clone_delete`, including crash/resume after every boundary. - trusted-clock tests attest every singleton column, automatic/operator null shape, deterministic proof digest, and transition mask; persist the exact raw-wall observation and operator-artifact hash; use only suspend-aware `CLOCK_BOOTTIME`; and prove `effective_now=MAX(old high-water,candidate)` at every gate/restart. A legal accepted lower-edge anchor below the old high-water cannot reopen a deadline between candidate and high-water. Same-ID `REPLACE`, deletion, partial proof-tuple change, and an unowned field update fail. - each in-process, offsite/B2, immutable pre-processing, Recall-preflight, deployment, and future repository-scanned producer preserves exact S26/S27 latches, accepts only exact attested S28, and removes every restricted root/control/cache/client fact through the frozen application → action/root scrub → VACUUM/check → external append/replication → required live purge/corroborating append → copy ledger/tombstone/final-attestation sequence. The requested backup either deletes the affected live root early through its ordinary purge or remains unpublished/fenced; restoring the sanitized copy cannot reuse that item ID even though live obligations/receipts were removed;
- backup fixtures seed unique sentinels in content/cache/artifacts/main/freelist/WAL/SHM/journal/staging/publication/uploader/CacheStorage/IndexedDB surfaces. The guard bypass succeeds only on a fresh volatile clone and fails on live/prior-live/restore-target/wrong-path/nonvolatile/hardlinked/wrong-runtime candidates. Plan tests close/hash the exact pre-plan clone, rebind the same device/inode, begin the transaction, arm its plan context, insert complete planned/action rows, then insert the application last; missing/extra/substituted/post-application planned/action rows, wrong closed hash/device/inode/base head/manifest/verifier kind, wrong nested PK/row/root preimages, aggregate count/hash, and planned-to-retired stable tuple fail. Fault injection crosses every plan commit, root scrub, the scrub-stage zero scan, pre/post-VACUUM full/quick/FK checks, the pre-finalization zero scan/evidence verification, external append and independent replication, lock-handoff checkpoint/reverse release/live-purge/corroborating append/canonical reacquisition/revalidation, and each exact final transaction step `per-record projection contexts/head -> copy-retired -> tombstone -> copy outbox -> arm pre-finalization context -> attestation`; every reordering, overlapping verifier context, omitted immediate/deferred FK parent, barrier failure, or rollback leaves no publishable partial attestation. Opposing backup/live-purge workers and injected contention prove the total lock/wait-for graph has no cycle and the complete handoff stays inside the measured sanitize budget. The plan-input and pre-finalization verifier paths each cross file/inode swap and every bound fact before consuming once. A pre-append failure leaves live state unchanged; any valid appended prefix remains authoritative, blocks publication, and forces exact live containment/purge. Empty and two-row aggregates/nested hashes are reproduced by independent encoders. Finalization then closes the file, independently hashes and checks it, runs the phase-bound post-finalization zero scan, and writes the detached attestation; the scrub-stage, pre-finalization, and post-finalization hashes are never required equal. Detached tests consume the full real-key-order fixture and independently alter attestation-ID alphabet/case, final file hash/size/page size/page count/freelist, full/quick/FK result, every post-finalization zero-scan surface token/count/order, each WAL/SHM/journal/temp absence token, JSON key order/whitespace/BOM/escape/final-brace/newline, key version, provisioning kind/receipt, and HMAC; every change fails. Cross-phase scan substitution, changing any persisted pre-finalization field without its evidence digest and one-shot verifier, changing the historical evidence/HMAC binding, and mutating the final database after its post-finalization scan all fail. Publication requires the active data-root-local signer, independent recovery verification, and exact authenticated provisioning-receipt agreement; recovery never exposes signing authority. Normal restore recomputes the post-finalization facts from the supplied closed file and verifies the authenticated historical pre-finalization binding without comparing the historical pre-finalization file or scan hash to that final file. Declared-root-loss restore may use only the independent historical verifier for input verification, but before any output plan row it must traverse `absent -> local_prepared -> recovery_verified -> receipt_committed -> active`, bind the fresh signer/key ID and exact root-loss provisioning receipt into application and detached bytes, sign only with that fresh local key, and have recovery verify the output. Crash/replay tests cross every state and receipt fsync; missing/wrong/forked historical evidence, operator artifact, provisioning lineage/receipt, fresh local signer, recovery acknowledgement, or independent ledger evidence fails. After secure delete/VACUUM/checks, restricted sentinels are absent and unrelated hashes remain;
- legacy/pre-028 YouTube items receive the exact all-null-component `legacy_unproven` contract and cannot gain a restricted source. A v1 item has all four server-read/service-worker/journal/extension component hashes and the exact established-clock digest; component nullness, order, digest, update, delete, UPSERT, and `REPLACE` substitutions fail. From its first response it returns only the content-free bootstrap to an old/no-proof service worker, never stores item HTML/API responses in any CacheStorage alias, never reads or writes full note content for that item in any exact current `brain-manual-notes-v2` state `dirty|in_flight|conflict|copy_only|acknowledged` or in extension/local storage, and deletes every same-item composite journal key by `itemId` index without reading or allowlisting state. Fixtures seed at least two editor instances for each current state plus historical `pending`, malformed, unknown, and future-state canaries; all same-item rows disappear while every other-item row remains byte-identical, and a blocked/aborted open, enumeration, delete, or commit denies completion. Current activation, guarded read/save, retry, restart, and concurrent clients cannot recreate a same-item row; bundle/SW/hash drift revokes reads. Route-by-route old/no-proof fixtures cover item HTML/RSC, library/list/search/FTS, collection/tag/topic/workflow/review/needs-upgrade, Ask/RAG/citations, notes/chat/cards, recent/pickers, JSON/bulk routes, and aggregate counts, proving the row is omitted before field serialization. Offline/restart/navigation-preload/redirect/response-clone fixtures prove a server purge receipt never relies on contacting an unreachable client;
- raw staging tests use at least two profiles whose copy, sanitize, watchdog-takeover, rename, physical-closure, and end-to-end destruction values are all non-equal and cross `copy_finish_by`, `sanitize_finish_by`, `raw_destroy_by`, each ordinary admission boundary `D-W-R-1|D-W-R|D-W-R+1`, each ordinary/final-rename boundary `D-R-1|D-R|D-R+1`, each fence admission boundary `raw_destroy_by-Q-1|raw_destroy_by-Q|raw_destroy_by-Q+1`, each fence pre-rename remaining-sequence boundary, each physical-closure `C-1|C|C+1` boundary, and earliest cleanup start at −1/0/+1 ms. Before any destination byte, they prove staging and both complete volatile registries share one exact canonical tmpfs mount/device/root inode with no bind or persistent backing, zero enabled swap, hibernation disabled, core dumps disabled for every raw-stage process, confined SQLite/temp directories, correct mode/owner, sufficient capacity, a separately isolated sole supervisor with OFD lease, and externally enforced pidfd takeover-completion plus rename bounds; persistent fallback, wrong/missing mount, swap, hibernation, core-dump, bind, capacity, dual supervisor, supervisor-group overlap, watchdog failure, unbounded reap/rename, uninterruptible holder, or identity drift fails the target capability before creation/readiness. Byte-exact tests cover every reservation and source-registry field/nullness/enum, record revision, transition request/receipt digest, successor-core digest, actor/edge/reason/role matrix, command/session/cgroup map, trusted-time conversion, both headrooms, both raw-destroy bounds, v2 VFS traces, complete v2 writer receipts/header-publication chains, semantic transcript/restart/match evidence, both zero-active cuts, terminal-token preimage, complete v3 audit bytes, idle/active/fencing/terminal shape, record hash/length, and old-or-new complete-record invariant. Before pruning, an old exact transition ID returns its retained receipt and current descendant after later transitions/restart; after pruning, only the exact prune ID/preimage is idempotent, every pre-prune ID returns `terminal-pruned`, a conflicting prune preimage rejects, and same-boot reservation-ID reuse remains denied;
- process/supervisor crash, `SIGKILL`, `SIGSTOP`, and response-loss injection crosses OFD acquisition/release, exclusive sibling create/write/fsync, predecessor/revision/hash/actor/edge/reason/role/headroom validation, watchdog arm, sole rename, broker-issued no-follow destination creation, device/inode binding, first byte, every bind/phase/unbind edge, source-registry begin/end/abort/prune, terminal accepted-read-epoch capture, later zero-active physical-attribution cut, replay/provisional handoff, priority fence, broker revocation, cgroup freeze/kill/reap, stable scans, unlink/parent fsync, destroyed rename, response, reconciliation, and pruning. An unrenamed sibling is never promoted; a renamed successor is authoritative and idempotently reconciled. Same-boot admission obtains only a supervisor-issued exact snapshot and enforces every active/fencing reservation. Power loss/reboot at every interval removes tmpfs bytes/records and permits readiness only after a fresh current-boot proof plus a newly mounted compliant empty staging/registry pair; no detailed reservation value may appear on any persistent surface. Under `global_maintenance -> source_admission_artifact_namespace`, admission permits a new restricted root only when its `retention_cleanup_start_by` is **strictly greater** than every active/fencing `raw_destroy_by`; `raw_destroy_by-1` and equality fail, `+1` alone may pass. Orphan/malformed/duplicate/expired/identity-mismatched records or stages deny admission and request fencing;
- fencing authenticates PID/start ticks, PGID/leader start ticks, role, command, session, and cgroup; installs absorbing denial without a canonical lock; atomically revokes the descriptor broker; freezes the reservation cgroup; proves tracked pidfd/membership completeness; kills/reaps every member; and only then performs stable all-process `/proc/[0-9]*/fd` scans by device/inode and canonical path—including deleted descriptors, directory FDs, database/WAL/SHM/journal/temp/sidecar entries—before and after fd-relative unlink/parent fsync through the supervisor-private staging-root management dirfd. Continuous open/openat/openat2, dup, fork, exec, cgroup-migration, `SCM_RIGHTS`, `/proc` reopen, and `pidfd_getfd` adversaries run throughout both scans and unlink. A post-revocation acquisition must be denied by the kernel; any descriptor outside the tracked cgroup is a capability breach and forbids `destroyed`. `SIGSTOP` of the writer while holding every canonical subset and `SIGSTOP` of an unrelated canonical-lock holder cannot delay raw destruction; only later content-free reconciliation/pruning may wait. Deleted-path FD, stale/reused PID or PGID, zombie, unauthenticated holder, hardlink, procfs denial/incompleteness, path-gone/inode-open, or either nonzero scan leaves the reservation `fencing`. The pre-scan excludes only the attested management root dirfd plus enumerated cleanup FDs; all leaf/short-lived FDs then close, and the post-scan excludes only that management dirfd. Terminal pruning occurs only after later canonical reconciliation plus an exact empty rescan. Slow online backup, partial destination, profile/device/headroom drift, worker/supervisor restart, ordinary error, scrub/VACUUM, copy/hardlink/upload, `SIGTERM`, and `SIGKILL` never publish/upload or outlive the immutable destruction bound on a supported target; a later/faster profile never extends it, and the measured margin covers watchdog restart, OFD takeover, reconciliation, broker revocation, kill/reap, both stable scans, unlink, and parent fsync;
- restore independently hashes/reclassifies/zero-scans the input and its exact detached attestation, covers candidate/prior-live/`.pre-restore`/sideline/target/temp originals, and either destroys each original or scrubs only a new volatile clone before destroying it. Restore an older S28 and exact S26/S27 against a nonempty newer retired ledger: every ledger-retired root in the candidate is scrubbed before publication, every externally corroborated tombstone is unioned, and the resulting set/head is monotonic. A candidate-only retired row yields exact `candidate_retired_tuple_unproven` and is never appended as new authority; a separately proven prior-live unmirrored row may use only the original-surface append path. Missing/forked/truncated local or independent ledger, failure at every append/union/rename barrier, pre-026, and historical unsanitized S28 all fail without exposing a listener; clock/profile crossing restarts the gate and no partial/unknown/mismatched surface starts a worker, timer, backup, or readiness;
- before 028 DDL, both independently supervised S27/S28 containment units, v2/profile/clock/lock registry, and capability latch are installed and execution-proven, and the exact first-layer, S27→S28 transition, and expected-S28 second-layer artifacts are verified before database open. Kill/failure after each transition action leaves the app down while containment still runs; a precommit fault returns exact S27, while a durable commit is accepted only through exact S28 reopen/no-op verification. Startup orders migration → fatal sweep/check → all other services; activation permits only pre-start pending with both units/time/profile proven and blocks partial/error/clock-latch states. Rollback never starts B27 or stops the fallback, and migration 028 remains outside the additive allowlist.

## 11. Advancement gate

The next step is an independent adversarial review of this addendum. A zero-unresolved-P0/P1 explicit GO authorizes implementation and packaging of only the two exact named source forks—`@ai-brain/better-sqlite3-authority@11.10.0-brain.1` and source-only `@ai-brain/sqlite-vec-authority@0.1.9-brain.1`—compiled solely into their one repository-owned native SQLite authority artifact, its immutable role wrapper, packaged capability tests, and every target's first-layer `native_authority_runtime_attestation_v1` in §6.12.1; it does not authorize migration SQL. A review must treat any ambiguous owner binding, lifetime, FK action, provenance backfill, revision trigger, R-032 transaction, or rollback behavior as a blocker rather than filling it in during implementation.

Migration 028 DDL may be authored only after both exact source forks, the sole combined native artifact, immutable role engine, all target first-layer artifacts/attestations, and the complete stock-negative/fork-positive vec0 plus role-seal capability matrix receive a separate zero-unresolved-P0/P1 implementation adversarial-review GO. After DDL is authored, every target's second-layer `schema_028_connection_capability_attestation_v1` must bind it to the reviewed native layer and exact vec0 shadow/trigger/statement manifests, and the one-way `schema_027_to_028_transition_capability_attestation_v1` must bind exact S27 schema/ledger authority plus the reviewed migration source/action/bound-value manifests to that expected S28 second layer. Migration 028 still cannot be prepared, run, or treated as ready until both target attestations, its exact source hash, S27 and S28 schema snapshots, full transition/no-op/crash test matrix, and a separate migration adversarial review pass. Browser/extension feature work, live access, held processing, production enablement, migration application, and deployment remain blocked.
